Use quando receber números de processo em formatos variados (com ou sem máscara, colados de sistemas diferentes), precisar validar dígitos verificadores, identificar tribunal, instância e órgão julgador, ou preparar dados para consulta em APIs judiciais (DataJud, e-SAJ, PJe, Escavador). - **cnki**: 知网检索与核对（乔淼PhD · AI学术训练营 · AI学术工作流）。用学员自己电脑上的浏览器查中国知网：按主题 / 篇名 / 关键词 / 作者 / 来源检索，可限年份、核心期刊（CSSCI / 北大核心 / CSCD 等）、只要学术期刊；按篇名核对一篇中文文献是否真实存在、作者 / 年份 / 刊名对不对；取题录详情（摘要、关键词、卷期页、DOI、GB/T 引文）；导出 RIS 或直接进 Zotero；在学员自己登录的窗口里下载中文 PDF。不用装 MCP，随 skill 附 scripts/cnki.mjs（要 Node.js 18 以上）。Trigger on："在知网搜一下"、"查知网"、"核对这篇中文文献是不是真的"、"知网检索 CSSCI 期刊"、"把知网结果导出到 Zotero"、"下载知网的 PDF"、"search CNKI"、"verify this Chinese paper on CNKI"。 ([qiaomiaojoe/ai-academic-workflow](https://github.com/qiaomiaojoe/ai-academic-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/cnki) · ★11)
- **cnki**: Search, browse, download and export papers (and patents, experimental) from CNKI 中国知网 via the user's own Chrome (chrome-devtools-mcp). 知网检索/下载 PDF CAJ/导出 GB-T 7714/推送 Zotero。Use when user says "知网", "CNKI", "中文文献", "cnki search", "知网下载", "知网专利", or wants Chinese academic literature. ([wyl010607/papersearch-skill](https://github.com/wyl010607/papersearch-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cnki) · ★6)
- **cnki**: Search and extract from CNKI (中国知网) — keyword search, advanced/filtered search (author/journal/date/source category SCI·EI·CSSCI·北大核心·CSCD), paper details (abstract/keywords/fund), journal lookup, journal indexing & impact factors, citation export (GB/T 7714 text + Markdown table), download a paper's PDF/CAJ, and save a paper's record into Zotero. Use when the user wants to find Chinese academic papers, read a paper's abstract/metadata, check whether a journal is core/indexed, export references, download a paper, or ingest a paper into Zotero. Does NOT bypass CAPTCHA. ([xunagent/cnki-codex-skill](https://github.com/xunagent/cnki-codex-skill/tree/HEAD/cnki) · ★2)
- **cnki-advanced-search**: Perform advanced search on CNKI with field filters like author, title, journal, date range, source category (SCI/EI/CSSCI/北大核心). - **cnki-advanced-search**: 知网（CNKI）高级检索论文自动化工具。当用户提供研究关键词或研究选题时，自动执行 三阶段检索：①主体联合检索（倒剥洋葱法，多组核心概念 AND 联合，获取直接相关文献）； ②独立补充检索（对各核心概念组分别单独检索，获取间接相关的背景文献与理论文献）； ③汇总合并（去重后按类别分色展示，输出统一 Excel 文件）。 触发条件：用户提到需要在知网/CNKI检索论文、高级检索、按关键词搜索CSSCI/C刊论文、 下载题录信息、获取论文摘要、按被引排序检索；或说"帮我在知网检索XX相关论文"、 "用知网高级检索搜索XX主题的C刊论文"、"帮我检索XX关键词的CSSCI论文"。 ([yipng05-max/-skills](https://github.com/yipng05-max/-skills/tree/HEAD/cnki-advanced-search) · ★333)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-architecture-design-for-spectral-data))

- **cnn-bioactivity-classification**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS data for many biological samples (e.g., 545 wild soybean accessions) plus a binary or binned bioactivity endpoint (DPPH, elastase, TFC, TPC) and want end-to-end activity prediction without compound annotation. - **cnn-chromatographic-peak-classification**: Use when raw LC-HRMS profile-mode chromatograms (local maxima in rt x mz space) must be separated into true chromatographic peaks versus irrelevant background signals, and peak-center/bounding-box annotations are needed for downstream feature grouping. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Unspecified classification dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy (%). - **cnn-model-training-for-tf-dna-binding**: Use when you have GC-matched ChIP-seq peak/background data (e.g. the 690 ENCODE datasets, 101-bp or 1001-bp windows) and need to train a CNN classifier of TF-DNA binding on sequence or DNAShape features. - **cnn-model-training-on-genomic-features**: Use when training supervised deep learning classifiers on ENCODE ChIP-seq peak data — either DNA sequence or DNA shape feature matrices — to later extract motifs and TFBSs, e.g. reproducing DESSO's 690-dataset benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-training-on-genomic-features))
- **cnn-model-training-with-bayesian-hyperparameter-optimization**: Use when predicting sgRNA on-target editing efficiency for CRISPR-mediated epigenome editing from one-hot encoded 40 bp sequences plus min-max normalized epigenetic channels (nucleosome positioning, ATAC-seq accessibility, DNA methylation, RNA expression), and hyperparameters must be optimized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-model-training-with-bayesian-hyperparameter-optimization))
- **cnn-multi-task-fingerprint-inference**: Use when the input is a 1H-13C HSQC spectrum (normal or multiplicity-edited) as a peak list with 1H/13C shift pairs, and the goal is to infer a fingerprint, molecular weight, and structure class for later similarity search against chemical databases. - **cnn-multitask-model-evaluation**: Use when a trained NMR-based CNN must be validated on a held-out HSQC test set by reproducing reported Top-1 accuracy, fingerprint cosine similarity, and molecular-weight R-squared metrics. - **cnn-rnn-architecture-ablation**: Use when you need to demonstrate that both the convolutional and recurrent layer sets of Chiron's CNN+RNN+CTC architecture are essential, by training full vs. ablated models on the same raw-signal tfrecord data and comparing read-level accuracy against a reference genome. - **cnn-sequence-model-inference**: Use when you need single-base-resolution functional impact scores (SAD = P(alt) − P(ref)) for non-coding SNPs across chromatin accessibility, histone mark, and TF binding profiles in a tissue-specific manner. - **cnn-sequence-model-training**: Use when a candidate list of polyA/IPA sites must be scored purely from nucleotide sequence context, or when a CNN classifier for polyA sites must be trained and benchmarked with AUROC. - **cnn-sgrna-activity-prediction**: Use when predicting sgRNA editing/knock-down or knock-up efficiency from a 40-nt target sequence plus genomic coordinates and epigenetic tracks (methylation, ATAC accessibility, RNA expression, nucleosome/TSS distance), e.g., screening dataset scoring or sgRNA design ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-sgrna-activity-prediction))

- **cnn-training-early-stopping**: Use when training a CNN classifier on one-hot encoded nucleotide sequences (e.g., 41-nt m6A windows) and you need a stable training regime with 5-fold cross-validation and early stopping. - **cnn-training-instance-augmentation**: Use when training a custom PeakBot CNN from LC-HRMS profile-mode chromatograms with ≥100 matched reference features, and you must synthesize augmented peak/background training instances with bounded decoy overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-training-instance-augmentation))
- **cnn-training-with-adam-optimizer**: Use when a pipeline needs to refine predicted splice-site positions from long-read (Iso-seq) alignments, or when reproducing TAGET's CNN splice-site correction trained on Ensembl-annotated exon/intron boundaries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-training-with-adam-optimizer))
- **cnn-transfer-learning-fine-tuning**: Use when ion images (2D spatial intensity maps) from imaging MS must be automatically classified as off-sample/on-sample, and a small curated labeled set exists that is too large to tag manually but suitable for fine-tuning a pretrained vision model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cnn-transfer-learning-fine-tuning))
- **cnn-transformer-hybrid-architecture-design**: Use when when you need to detect and classify peaks in LC-MS regions of interest (ROIs) and simultaneously localize their boundaries for area integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cnn-transformer-hybrid-architecture-design) · 3 copies)
- **cnn-variant-effect-prediction**: Use when you need to score the reference vs. alternative allele of a non-coding GWAS variant against chromatin profiles (DNase, histone marks, TF binding) in a tissue-specific manner, or to identify the motif driving a predicted effect (e.g., NFE2 at rs6426833). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cnn-variant-effect-prediction))
- **cnn-variant-quality-scoring**: Use when Oxford Nanopore long-read SV candidate calls from the six ConsensuSV-ONT callers need a quality score, i.e. candidate variants must be filtered via image-encoded evidence into a CNN probability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cnn-variant-quality-scoring))
- **cnn_allocator**: CNN pilot scorer sub-project (Model A): train_cnn_pilot_allocator.py, inference_cnn_pilot_allocator.py, TDL-A closed-loop eval, checkpoints/data caches. Use when editing CNN training/inference, PilotScorerModelA, CNNPilotSampler, or environment.yml. For vec(H)/LMMSE/pilot conventions use model-based-gaussian skill. ([tal-pardo/model_based_pilot_allocation](https://github.com/tal-pardo/model_based_pilot_allocation/tree/HEAD/old/.cursor/skills/cnn_allocator))
- **cnndm-eval**: Evaluates abstractive summarization quality by scoring generated summaries against human-written references and expert rubric-based scores. It measures how well automatic metrics correlate with human judgments across different summarization systems. Use when the user wants to benchmark on CNNDM, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports COMET. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/cnndm-eval) · ★3)
